### [1] [Searches for GeV-Scale ALPs at RHIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07739)
*Kaori Fuyuto,Claudio Andrea Manzari,Hitoshi Murayama*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用RHIC上Au+Au超外围碰撞数据，通过γγ→a→γγ共振过程搜寻类轴子粒子（ALP），在2–5 GeV质量区间对ALP-光子耦合常数g_{aγγ}给出约4×10⁻⁴ GeV⁻¹的上限，覆盖未被探索的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展ALP参数空间探测范围，特别是中等质量（GeV量级）且弱耦合区域，利用重离子碰撞中Z⁴增强的双光子亮度和RHIC低光子能量阈值优势。

Method: 基于PHENIX实验已采集的1.9 nb⁻¹超外围Au+Au碰撞数据，模拟ALP信号过程γγ→a→γγ及主要背景（轻强子散射、强子共振产生、误识别e⁺e⁻对），进行事例率估算与统计极限分析。

Result: 在m_a ≈ 2–5 GeV区间获得g_{aγγ} ≳ 4×10⁻⁴ GeV⁻¹的95% CL上限，首次覆盖该质量-耦合参数区域；高亮度数据可显著提升灵敏度。

Conclusion: RHIC超外围重离子碰撞是探测GeV尺度ALP的有效新平台，建议开展PHENIX及其他实验的专用数据分析以拓展ALP搜寻前沿。

Abstract: We point out that ultra-peripheral Au+Au collision data collected at the Relativistic Heavy Ion Collider, operational during 2000-2026, can be used to search for axion-like particles coupled to photons via the resonant process $γγ\to a \to γγ$. Exploiting the $Z^4$ enhancement of the two-photon luminosity in heavy-ion collisions and the low photon energy thresholds achievable at RHIC, we simulate signal and background processes, the latter dominated by light-by-light scattering, hadronic resonance production, and misidentified $e^+e^-$ pairs, and estimate upper limits on the ALP-photon coupling $g_{aγγ}$ assuming $1.9~\text{nb}^{-1}$ of existing data collected by the PHENIX experiment. We find sensitivity to ALP masses in the range $2~\text{GeV} \lesssim m_a \lesssim 5~\text{GeV}$ with couplings $g_{aγγ} \gtrsim 4\times 10^{-4}~\text{GeV}^{-1}$, probing previously unexplored regions of parameter space. Access to larger luminosity datasets could substantially extend the sensitivity of this search, motivating a dedicated analysis of ultra-peripheral collision data collected at RHIC by PHENIX as well as other experiments.

</details>


### [2] [Impact of $N^*$ and $Λ^*$ resonances on $CP$ violation in $Λ_b^0$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08248)
*Yu-Kuo Hsiao,Kai-Lei Wang,Juan Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在组分夸克模型框架下，系统分析了Λ_b^0→pK^-π^+π^-四体衰变中通过N*和Λ*共振态（如N(1535)、N(1520)、Λ(1670)、Λ(1690)等1P波激发态）主导的两体中间过程，成功复现了实验观测到的分支比与CP不对称度A_CP≈3.18%，为首个强子CP破坏现象提供了微观机制解释，并建立了激发重子共振态影响多体美重子衰变的普适理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LHCb首次观测到的重子CP破坏现象（Λ_b^0→pK^-π^+π^-），厘清其底层两体共振过程Λ_b^0→N^*M和Λ_b^0→Λ^*M中激发核子N*与超子Λ*共振态的具体贡献。

Method: 采用组分夸克模型（Constituent Quark Model），识别并计算1P波重子激发态（包括N(1535), N(1520), Λ(1670), Λ(1690)等）对两体中间过程的贡献，结合相空间积分与共振参数拟合，提取整体四体衰变的分支比与CP不对称度。

Result: 得到分支比B(Λ_b^0→pK^-π^+π^-)=(30.0^{+2.8+4.0}_{-1.3-3.4}±1.8)×10^{-6}，CP不对称度A_CP=(3.18±0.11±0.13±0.11)%，与实验一致；确认N(1535)、Λ(1670)等1P态是主导共振源。

Conclusion: 首次构建了激发重子共振态影响多体美重子衰变的定量理论框架，证明重子CP破坏主要源于N*/Λ*共振态的干涉与衰变振幅的弱相位差，该机制具有普适性，可推广至其他重子CP不对称研究。

Abstract: The four-body decay $Λ_b^0\to pK^-π^+π^-$ has led to the first observation of baryonic $CP$ violation. However, the underlying subprocesses $Λ_b^0\to N^* M$ and $Λ_b^0\to Λ^* M$, as well as the roles of excited nucleon ($N^*$) and hyperon ($Λ^*$) resonances, remain largely unexplored. Within the constituent quark model, we identify the relevant resonant states contributing to these underlying two-body transitions, including $N(1535)$, $N(1520)$, $Λ(1670)$, $Λ(1690)$, together with the remaining $1P$-wave baryon states. We obtain the resonant branching fraction ${\cal B}(Λ_b^0\to pK^-π^+π^-) =(30.0^{+2.8+4.0}_{-1.3-3.4}\pm1.8)\times10^{-6}$, while the resulting ${\cal A}_{CP}(Λ_b^0\to pK^-π^+π^-)=(3.18\pm0.11\pm0.13\pm0.11)\%$ provides a natural interpretation of the first observed baryonic $CP$ asymmetry. Our analysis establishes the first comprehensive framework for quantifying the impact of excited baryon resonances in multi-body beauty-baryon decays, with the associated mechanism generally applicable to baryonic $CP$ asymmetries.

</details>


### [3] [Meson-Nucleus Bound States with Neural-Network Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09254)
*Wei-Lin Wu,Liang-Zhen Wen,Shi-Lin Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次系统计算了φ、η_c和J/ψ介子与原子核（至A=12）形成束缚态的基态性质，基于HAL QCD在近物理点给出的介子-核子势，采用推广的神经网络变分蒙特卡洛方法求解(A+1)体薛定谔方程；发现不同介子存在不同的最小成键质量数（A≥2,4,6），结合能尺度从数十MeV（φ）到亚MeV（η_c）不等，且核子分布随A增大由晕结构向内嵌结构演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立格点QCD计算出的介子-核子相互作用与实验可观测的介子-原子核多体束缚态之间的定量桥梁，并预测轻核区强子分子态存在的可能性。

Method: 基于HAL QCD在近物理点得到的φN、η_c N、J/ψN两体力，构建有效介子-核子势；将神经网络变分蒙特卡洛（NN-VMC）方法推广至含显式介子自由度的(A+1)体体系，直接求解全量子多体薛定谔方程。

Result: 发现φ-核在A≥2即形成强束缚态（数十MeV），J/ψ-核在A≥4、η_c-核在A≥6才出现弱束缚（几MeV及亚MeV）；φ-核结合能每核子呈非单调变化，在⁴He处达峰；所有系统中核子密度分布均被介子压缩，且介子空间构型由晕态（low-A）渐变为内嵌态（high-A）。

Conclusion: 介子-核束缚态的存在性与强度由介子-核子相互作用的短程吸引强度决定；φ介子因强标量耦合表现出最显著的束缚效应；该工作为未来J-PARC、PANDA、EIC等实验探测重味介子核提供了首个第一性原理预言基准。

Abstract: We present the first systematic calculations of $φ$-, $η_c$-, and $J/ψ$-nucleus ground states up to mass number $A{=}12$ based on the HAL QCD meson-nucleon potentials at near-physical point. The $(A{+}1)$-body Schrödinger equation is solved with a neural-network variational Monte Carlo framework, generalized to incorporate mesonic degrees of freedom. Benchmarking on light nuclei from $^2$H to $^{12}$C yields ground-state energies consistent with experiment. Meson-nucleus bound states emerge at $A\ge2$ for $φ$, $A\ge4$ for $J/ψ$, and $A\ge6$ for $η_c$. The $φ$-nucleus systems exhibit the strongest binding, with binding energies reaching tens of MeV. The $J/ψ$-nucleus and $η_c$-nucleus systems are weakly bound at the few-MeV and sub-MeV scale, respectively. The binding energy per nucleon deepens nearly linearly with $A$ for charmonium systems, whereas the $φ$-nucleus system exhibits a non-monotonic behavior peaking at $^4$He -- a distinctive hallmark of the short-range and strongly attractive $φN$ interaction. The meson compresses the nucleon distribution relative to the parent nucleus, and evolves from a halo configuration to one embedded inside the nucleus with increasing $A$. Our results provide predictions for future experimental searches, and establish a quantitative bridge between lattice QCD meson-nucleon interactions and the emergent many-body phenomena in meson-nucleus bound states.

</details>


### [4] [Core Composition Effects on the QCD Axion Mass Limit from Neutron Star Cooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07742)
*Fernando Arias-Aragón,Federico Nola*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在中子星核心中引入超子和Δ共振态等重粒子后，对QCD轴子（KSVZ与DFSZ模型）质量上限的影响；结果表明该影响总体较弱，从而增强了现有轴子质量限制的可靠性，并指出DFSZ模型下该限制可能落入IAXO实验灵敏度窗口，使轴子探测具备探针中子星物态方程的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统中子星轴子冷却限界通常仅考虑核子与轻子组分，但极端致密环境下超子与Δ共振态可能真实存在，需评估其对轴子质量限界的影响。

Method: 采用多种允许超子与Δ共振态出现的现代核物质状态方程（EoS），结合轴子-核子耦合机制，重新计算中子星内部轴子产生率与能量损失率，进而修正KSVZ与DFSZ模型下的轴子质量上限。

Result: 引入重强子自由度后，轴子质量上限变化总体温和（<20%）；DFSZ模型下上限可移至IAXO当前设计灵敏度区间（~0.02–0.25 eV）。

Conclusion: 现有基于纯核子EoS的轴子质量限界具有稳健性；若IAXO在未来探测到该能区轴子信号，其谱形与通量可反演中子星核心是否含超子或Δ态，实现天体物理与粒子物理交叉诊断。

Abstract: Neutron stars are very dense media in which axions may be produced. This has been used to set limit on the QCD axion mass, usually under the assumption that only neutrons, protons, electrons, and muons appear in the star core. Given the extreme conditions reached within neutron stars, it is reasonable to consider that other particles, such as hyperons and $Δ$ resonances, may exist on-shell. In this work, we study how the limit on the mass of QCD axions, namely KSVZ and DFSZ invisible axions, is altered when different equations of state are used, allowing for heavier particles to appear in the neutron star core. We find that this dependence is in general mild and thus reinforces the reliability of the known limit. Additionally, in the DFSZ scenario, it may drive the limit within the sensitivity window for IAXO. This would allow this experiment to discern the composition of neutron star cores if an axion were to be observed within that window.

</details>


### [5] [Space- and time-like electromagnetic form factors of the $\mathbfΩ$ baryon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09324)
*L. Albino,B. Almeida-Zamora,A. Bashir,J. J. Cobos-Martínez,K. Raya,J. Segovia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在基于矢量⊗矢量接触相互作用的禁闭、对称性保持框架下，利用QCD的Dyson-Schwinger方程彩虹 ladder截断与Poincaré协变Faddeev方程，计算了全奇异Ω重子（sss）的弹性电磁形状因子（含电单极、磁偶极、电四极、磁八极），并系统分析其在类空与类时区域的行为及其对 dressed-quark 反常磁矩的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ω baryon（sss）是检验夸克质量效应与SU(3)味对称性破缺对强子结构影响的理想探针；需在满足规范协变性（Ward-Takahashi恒等式）的前提下，自洽构造电磁流并获得完整多极展开。

Method: 采用rainbow-ladder截断下的Dyson-Schwinger方程描述夸克传播子，结合Poincaré协变Faddeev方程求解Ω三夸克束缚态；电磁流严格满足Ward-Takahashi恒等式；通过渐近解析延拓将类空解外推至类时区域。

Result: 得到四个独立电磁多极形状因子（G_E0, G_M1, G_E2, G_M3）在宽动量转移范围内的数值结果；静态电磁矩及高阶多极项显著依赖于dressed夸克的反常磁矩；类时有效形状因子可与实验及唯象分析对比。

Conclusion: 该对称性保持框架能自洽描述Ω重子的电磁结构；dressed夸克反常磁矩是调控其磁性与高阶多极响应的关键非微扰参数；类时延拓方案为连接理论预测与e+e−→ΩΩ¯等过程的实验测量提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present predictions for the elastic electromagnetic form factors of the $Ω$ baryon in both space-like and time-like regions, computed within a confining, symmetry-preserving framework based on a vector$\,\otimes\,$vector contact interaction. The calculation is performed in the rainbow-ladder truncation of QCD's Dyson-Schwinger equations, combined with a Poincaré-covariant Faddeev equation for the three-quark bound state. The $Ω$ baryon, composed solely of strange quarks, provides a particularly clean environment in which to investigate the role of quark mass and SU(3)-flavor symmetry in shaping baryon structure. Within this approach, the electromagnetic current is constructed consistently with the Ward-Takahashi identity, yielding four independent form factors associated with the electric monopole, magnetic dipole, electric quadrupole, and magnetic octupole moments. We compute these form factors over a broad kinematic domain and analyze their behavior in both space-like and time-like regions. The resulting static electromagnetic moments and multipole form factors of the $Ω$ baryon exhibit a visible sensitivity to the dressed-quark anomalous magnetic moment, particularly in the magnetic and higher-order multipole sectors. The time-like form factors are obtained through asymptotic analytic continuation of the corresponding space-like solutions, allowing the construction of the effective form factor and its comparison with available experimental data and recent phenomenological analyses.

</details>


### [6] [Machine learning unveils the quark mass dependence of the pseudoscalar meson decay constants in three-flavour N$^2$LO ChPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08609)
*Zejian Zhuang,Fernando Gil Domínguez,Raquel Molina*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用LASSO机器学习方法分析了最新格点QCD数据，精确确定了三味N²LO手征微扰论中π、K和η介子衰变常数对夸克质量的依赖关系，直至π介子质量约780 MeV（接近SU(3)极限），并以此为输入在协变重子手征微扰论框架下预言了SU(3)极限下八重态重子的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确刻画赝标量介子衰变常数f_π, f_K, f_η对夸克质量的依赖关系，为低能QCD有效理论提供可靠输入，并检验手征展开在较高质量区间的收敛性与适用性。

Method: 采用LASSO（Least Absolute Shrinkage and Selection Operator）稀疏回归机器学习方法，拟合近期三味格点QCD计算数据，在N²LO三味手征微扰论（ChPT）框架下提取低能常数（LECs），特别是与夸克质量依赖相关的Gasser-Leutwyler系数。

Result: 成功确定了f_π, f_K, f_η在m_π ≈ 780 MeV（即SU(3)对称性破缺显著但仍可控区域）内的夸克质量依赖行为；给出了N²LO ChPT中关键低能常数的高精度约束；并基于此预测了协变BChPT中八重态重子（如p, n, Λ, Σ, Ξ）在SU(3)极限下的质量谱。

Conclusion: LASSO方法可有效提升LECs的提取精度，验证了N²LO ChPT在m_π ~ 780 MeV仍具良好描述能力；该夸克质量依赖关系可作为强子谱、弱衰变、CKM矩阵元等低能强相互作用计算的关键先验输入。

Abstract: The quark mass dependence of the pseudoscalar meson decay consntants, $f_π, f_K$ and $f_η$, are determined from three-flavor N$^2$LO ChPT till pion masses around $780$ MeV, near the SU(3) limit. This is done by conducting an analysis of recent LQCD data using the LASSO method, a machine-learning technique which allows to pin down the relevant low-energy-constants with high precision. Since the pion decay constant is a fundamental quantity which usually appears in relevant phenomenological lagrangians or Effective Field Theories based on QCD at low energies, this analysis can be used as input to evaluate the quark mass dependence of hadronic states. As an example, we predict the masses of the octect baryons in the SU(3) limit within covariant Baryon Chiral Perturbation Theory.

</details>


### [7] [Detector performance at SHiP for cascade-produced long-lived particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07743)
*Matei Climescu,Yehor Kyselov,Maksym Ovchynnikov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了SHiP实验中厚靶级联产生对轻长寿命粒子（LLPs）产额的影响，发现尽管级联可增加LLP数目，但因其动量软、重建效率低，实际可观测事例率被显著压低；对光子亲和型ALP和重惰性中微子（HNL）两种情形分别量化了该压制效应，并探讨了通过放宽选事标准或引入活性靶子探测器来部分恢复事例率的可能方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 级联产生可能增强SHiP实验中轻LLPs的事例数，但其软动量特性导致探测器接受度与重建效率严重受限，需定量评估其真实贡献。

Method: 结合半解析事例率计算与电磁量能器中ALP重建的探测器级模拟，对光子亲和型ALP（来自电磁级联）和重惰性中微子HNL（来自次级K介子衰变）两类LLP进行系统分析。

Result: 对于标称SHiP探测器设计：(1) 级联ALP仅在极轻质量区带来中等增强，质量升高后贡献迅速压低至次主导或可忽略；(2) HNL来自次级K介子时，仅考虑子粒子几何接受度即已使其级联贡献次主导；(3) 提出放宽事件筛选条件与增设活性靶子探测器可部分恢复级联事例率。

Conclusion: 级联产生虽提升LLP产额，但受限于软谱与重建瓶颈，在SHiP当前设计下对ALP和HNL的探测灵敏度提升有限；优化探测策略（如活性靶、宽松重建）是释放其潜力的关键。

Abstract: Previous studies have shown that cascade production in the thick target of the SHiP experiment may substantially enhance the number of light long-lived particles (LLPs) decaying in the fiducial volume. However, cascade-produced LLPs are typically soft, so daughter-level acceptance and reconstruction effects can strongly suppress the observable event rate. We quantify this suppression for two representative cases: photophilic axion-like particles produced in electromagnetic cascades, and heavy neutral leptons produced in decays of secondary kaons. We combine a semi-analytic event-rate calculation with a detector-level study of ALP reconstruction in the electromagnetic calorimeter. For the nominal SHiP detector design, cascade ALPs give at most a moderate enhancement over primary production, and only at the lightest masses; at higher masses, the cascade contribution becomes subdominant or negligible. For HNLs from secondary kaons, the cascade contribution is already subdominant after imposing daughter-level geometric acceptance. We also identify possible ways to recover part of the cascade event rate, including relaxed event-selection criteria and an active-target subdetector.

</details>


### [8] ["Hadron-in-fat-jet'' AI Tagging to Detect Rare Decays Such as $W^{\pm}\toπ^{\pm}γ$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09458)
*Linrui Chen,Tianyi Yang,Zixun Kou,Zijian Wang,Youpeng Wu,Leyun Gao,Qiang Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种新型LHC探测范式——'强子嵌入大R喷注'（hadron-in-fat-jet），以搜索稀有衰变W±→π±γ，利用定制化Sophon AI模型、事件级BDT与软脱落质量谱拟合，对450 fb⁻¹数据给出分支比上限ℬ < 2.78×10⁻⁵（95% CL）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破传统多原初子喷注子结构分析的局限，探索罕见或半排他性衰变中局域强子/夸克偶素信号嵌入高pT大R喷注的新物理探针，拓展对轻/奇异共振态及稀有标准模型过程的灵敏度。

Method: 采用针对大R喷注优化的预训练Sophon AI模型进行签名导向微调，结合事件级梯度提升决策树（BDT）与软脱落质量分布的似然拟合，实现背景抑制与信号提取。

Result: 在450 fb⁻¹积分亮度下，获得W±→π±γ分支比95%置信水平上限为2.78×10⁻⁵；首次实现了'强子嵌入大R喷注'范式的实证验证。

Conclusion: 该范式具备普适性，可推广至其他稀有SM过程及轻/奇异共振态搜寻；未来通过改进触发策略、拓展产生道与专用标记器，灵敏度将显著提升。

Light dark particles can be produced in two-body decays of Standard Model particles, giving rise to missing energy signals that can not only be observed in high-precision flavor experiments, but also be probed in core-collapse supernovae and the cosmic microwave background. Abstract: Resonant $Z$-boson production is a cornerstone of precision electroweak physics, with its vacuum line shape set by the $Z$ mass, width, and collision kinematics. We show that a strong laser field can significantly alter this picture. By treating the field nonperturbatively, we find that laser dressing of the incoming fermions alters the effective collision kinematics and opens laser-photon exchange channels, including multiphoton processes, in $e^{+}e^{-}$ collisions. As a result, the $Z$-resonance profile develops distinct intensity-dependent regimes, evolving from the vacuum limit to saturation at intermediate field strengths and to an approximately quadratic enhancement at higher intensities. Additionally, the polarization composition of the produced $Z$ bosons is redistributed. In particular, at high intensities the laser-induced contribution can compensate the intrinsic chiral asymmetry of the electroweak interaction, leading to nearly parity-balanced $Z$-boson production. Our results identify that strong classical fields can dynamically control electroweak resonance phenomena, opening a bridge between strong-field QED and high-energy collider physics.

Abstract: Current observations constrain primordial perturbations to be adiabatic, approximately Gaussian, and nearly-scale invariant. However, a generic mixed state satisfying these constraints has additional unconstrained degrees of freedom, which can be parameterized by the purity of the state and its momentum variance. This allowable parameter space reveals a unified geometric landscape of mixed states, allowing us to map and relate distinct models of decoherence and their respective pointer bases. Crossing the threshold of a regular, positive-definite Glauber-Sudarshan $P$-function requires the environment to actively inject momentum into the system, rather than suppress it. This enhanced momentum variance dynamically sources the decaying mode of the gravitational potential in the radiation era. While the decaying mode vanishes fast enough to preserve the temporal coherence of the Cosmic Microwave Background, its initial amplitude places severe theoretical constraints on decoherence models to avoid gravitational non-linearities. These non-linearity bounds definitively rule out decohered thermal states, while long-wavelength divergences restrict amplitude-diagonal decoherence models to fewer than 70 $e$-folds of inflation. Altogether, we present a unifying framework for evaluating the quantum-to-classical transition of the early universe.

Abstract: We show that generic Maxwell fields can be represented within the matter-space framework by introducing two independent matter-space flows. The minimal completion is obtained by writing $$F=G^{(1)}+G^{(2)},$$ where each sector field $G^{(I)}$ is the pull-back of a matter-space two-form from an independent flow. Each sector is individually degenerate, $G^{(I)}\wedge G^{(I)}=0$, while the full field satisfies $F\wedge F=2G^{(1)}\wedge G^{(2)}$; the invariant excluded by the one-flow theory is thus recovered as an inter-flow quantity. We interpret this structure in terms of mutual helicity: the total helicity decomposes into two self-helicity contributions and a mutual term whose exterior derivative is $F\wedge F$. Abstract: Direct detections of gravitational waves provide a unique opportunity to probe the astrophysical origin of compact binary mergers. Here, we re-derive the line-of-sight acceleration effects and implement them in state-of-the-art quasi-circular waveform models with precession and higher-order modes. We find no substantial evidence for line-of-sight acceleration in these events. Abstract: We revisit quantum field theory in curved spacetime (QFTCS) as a semi-classical framework for quantum matter on classical geometries, emphasizing its limitations, including vacuum ambiguity and background dependence. Thus, the statement that "Weyl metrics do not allow $Λ\neq 0$ " is precise only when the metric is assumed to be in canonical Weyl coordinates. Abstract: Extreme mass ratio inspirals (EMRIs) are among the primary targets for space-based gravitational-wave (GW) detectors, providing valuable opportunities to study stellar-mass compact objects orbiting supermassive black holes (SMBHs) and to probe gravity in the strong-field regime. As the LISA, TAIJI, and TianQin missions are expected to operate around 2035, joint observations by multiple detectors may provide enhanced measurement capabilities compared to individual missions. In this work, we investigate the detectability and parameter-constraint prospects for EMRIs using global detector networks composed of LISA, TianQin, and two alternative TAIJI orbital configurations. Employing Fisher information matrix analysis, we find that joint observations improve source localization relative to a standalone LISA mission, with sky-localization uncertainties reduced by up to two orders of magnitude for a one-month observation period. We further find that a three-detector network (LISA-TAIJI-TianQin) operating for one month yields parameter constraints comparable to, and in some cases tighter than, those obtained from a one-year observation by LISA alone. Abstract: We analyze a class of linear Ricci--trace deformations of Einstein's field equations in which the relative weight between the Ricci tensor and the scalar-curvature trace sector is modified while the metric remains the only gravitational field. The purpose of the analysis is structural rather than phenomenological: we classify the corresponding field-equation class, fix the parameter dictionaries commonly used in the Rastall-gravity literature, and identify which equivalence statements survive after Newtonian calibration. We show that two frequently used parametrizations, \[ (1-ε)R_{μν}-\frac12 g_{μν}R=κ_εT_{μν}, \qquad R_{μν}-\frac{1-λ}{2}g_{μν}R=κ_λT_{μν}, \] are algebraically isomorphic only if both the deformation parameter and the bare gravitational coupling are transformed simultaneously. This algebraic equivalence, however, is not automatically an operational equivalence. Once the same laboratory stress tensor and the same measured Newton constant are fixed, the parameter map is a passive reparametrization only at the Einstein point. We further identify the \(λ\)-representative with the standard Rastall equation, clarify the role of the conserved effective source, derive the corresponding FLRW perfect-fluid sector, and discuss degenerate cases including vacuum, trace-free matter, radiation, dust, and the singular traceless point. Finally, we distinguish the Ricci--trace class from Unimodular Gravity (UG): although both involve the trace sector, UG follows from a restricted variational principle and produces the cosmological constant as an integration constant, rather than from an algebraic Ricci--trace deformation. The result is a compact operational classification of Rastall-type Ricci--trace models.

The inverse map tests when a conditioned drift is compatible with a scalar operational loss surface and reconstructs its boundary-normal Doob class. The construction therefore gives a stochastic survival interface between quantum-gravity control data and the histories that remain on the landscape side of control.

Abstract: We present classical solutions for magnetic monopoles and dyons induced by global monopoles for string-inspired models, in the presence of gravity. Two distinct scenarios are analyzed. In the first, magnetic monopoles arise from the coupling of a non-trivial massless dilaton field to the electromagnetic (EM) sector. In the second, dyonic solutions emerge in the presence of a non-trivial Kalb-Ramond (KR) axion field and a massless dilaton field, which couples the KR and EM sectors. In both models, the monopole and dyon configurations originate from a global monopole associated with the spontaneous breaking of a global O(3) symmetry. The EM sector is described by Born-Infeld electrodynamics, ensuring regularity of the fields at the core of the monopole and a finite self-energy. The resulting solutions represent particle-like configurations with a well-defined core and positive ADM (Arnowitt-Deser-Misner) mass, and are shown to satisfy all standard energy conditions. We also demonstrate the existence of a minimum nonzero magnetic charge in the limiting case of a magnetic monopole with vanishing mass. We also discuss stability criteria for our magnetic-monopole and dyon solutions. We first demonstrate that the mechanical-stability criteria for these solutions are satisified. These require the finiteness of the total force components and also the outward-pointing nature of the radial component, which indicates the avoidance of collapse. Next we analyse the dynamical (linear perturbative) stability of the self-gravitating Born-Infeld monopole and dyon solutions within Einstein-Born--Infeld-dilaton-axion theory in the Gervalle-Volkov framework, and demonstrate that both monopole and dyonic configurations are linearly stable against electromagnetic perturbations in the exterior region, with dyons exhibiting a birefringent helicity structure due to axion-induced mixing.

Abstract: For over four decades, Gribov's no-pole condition has been almost exclusively explored in Euclidean space, where the elliptic nature of the Faddeev-Popov operator provides a clear spectral boundary. In physical Minkowski spacetime, however, this operator becomes a hyperbolic wave operator, and the Euclidean positivity criterion collapses. We show that the natural Lorentzian replacement is a real-time boundary-value problem: a gauge configuration remains inside the first Gribov region as long as the Faddeev-Popov wave equation admits no source-free solutions obeying the Feynman boundary condition. For backgrounds localized in time, this condition translates to the injectivity of the negative-frequency block of the classical ghost scattering map. For stationary backgrounds, it becomes a spatial bound-state or threshold-resonance problem under Fourier transformation. Using a Wronskian identity, we prove that pure frequency mixing in stable self-adjoint time-dependent channels is structurally protected and cannot by itself produce the obstruction. While static chromomagnetic backgrounds reproduce the familiar zero-frequency horizon crossing, static chromoelectric potentials reach the horizon at finite, non-vanishing frequency - a uniquely Lorentzian phenomenon arising because $A_0$ couples directly to the ghost time derivative. We also cast the condition in Fredholm form and show that the exact restriction is a functional determinant, which the naive local continuation of the Gribov-Zwanziger action fails to reproduce, leaving the construction of a genuine local real-time action as the central open problem.

</details>

Abstract: We study the post-inflationary open-string Hagedorn phase in perturbatively moduli stabilized brane-antibrane inflation. In this class of models, the volume modulus is stabilized by perturbative corrections rather than by non-perturbative effects to the superpotential, thereby avoiding the standard brane-antibrane $η$-problem. Since inflation ends through tachyon condensation and brane-antibrane annihilation, the endpoint is intrinsically stringy and need not be described immediately by an ordinary radiation bath. We analyze whether the energy released at annihilation can drive the visible sector into an open-string Hagedorn phase, and study the consequences for dark radiation. If the Standard Model (SM) branes lie in the same throat as where the annihilation occurs, we find that a modest fraction of the released energy deposited into surviving visible open strings is sufficient to enter the Hagedorn regime and can suppress the effective number of relativistic species $ΔN_{\rm eff}$ below current observational bounds. If the SM lies in a different throat, the result depends on inter-throat energy transfer: prompt or delayed transfer can still yield a visible Hagedorn phase. However, the mechanism is most efficient when the local string scale in the SM throat is lower than or comparable to that in the annihilation throat.

</details>

Motivation: 统一几何热力学与信息几何，并拓展Souriau热力学至更一般的Kähler微观事件流形（特别是Calabi–Vesentini流形），以构建具有明确几何结构和对称性破缺机制的统计物理框架，服务于曲率空间中的机器学习（如Cartan神经网络）。

Method: 在热力学接触流形上构造Reeb协变度量，使其在等熵辛子流形上拉回为Kähler度量，在平衡态拉格朗日子流形上拉回为Fisher信息度量；采用Special Kähler几何刻画Calabi–Vesentini流形，结合紧致阿贝尔群作用与Killing矩映射，利用Tits–Satake分类实现配分函数的显式积分；引入非线性Casimir函数作为对称性破缺序参量。

Conclusion: 几何热力学与信息几何可通过Contact–Kähler–Lagrangian三层结构统一；Souriau框架可自然延拓至Special Kähler范畴，其对称性破缺由非线性Casimir函数刻画；该理论为非欧机器学习提供严格微分几何基础，标志着统计物理与深度学习交叉的范式升级。

Abstract: In this paper we clarify the relation between Geometric Thermodynamics and Information Geometry based on the Fisher matrix. On the macroscopic odd-dimensional contact manifold of thermodynamic variables, we introduce for the first time a metric, whose pull-back on the isoentropic symplectic submanifolds transverse to the Reeb field is Kählerian. The pull-back of such metric on equilibrium states, that are lagrangian submanifolds, is the Fisher Hessian. Then we consider the Souriau-like Thermodynamics that uses Calabi-Vesentini (CV) manifolds as Kaehlerian microscopic event manifolds and the Killing moment maps as observable functions. A systematic use of the theory of compact abelian structures and the setup of Special Kähler Geometry in which CV manifolds are encoded allows us to perform the explicit integration defining the partition function for any entry in the CV Tits Satake universality class. The additional actions completing the abelian structure are non linear Casimir functions of the Killing moment-maps and suggest a generalization of Souriau thermodynamics that partially breaks the isometry group symmetry by means of the non vanishing mean values of the Casimir functions in a manner similar to the spontaneous magnetization in ferromagnetism. Our new exact Gibbs distributions provide the analogue for Cartan Neural Networks of the Gaussian probability distributions in flat space used in conventional Machine Learning.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 应用紧致SO(2) S-对偶变换于旋转Einstein-标量种子解，导出NS-NS扇区（g_{μν}, B_{μν}, φ）的精确解析解；结合PPN展开分析静态极限的多极结构；研究Kerr视界处曲率行为与极轴上度规分量的奇异性分布，识别O(D,D)协变量的正则性。

Conclusion: 该解为Double Field Theory中非黎曼几何的物理实现提供了首个具体候选背景：在传统度规失效的极轴上，O(D,D)基本变量{d,ℋ_AB}仍保持良好定义，表明DFT框架天然容纳此类退化几何。

Abstract: We construct an exact rotating vacuum of the NS--NS sector, equivalently a Double Field Theory vacuum. The construction applies compact $\mathbf{SO}(2)$ S-duality to a rotating Einstein--scalar seed. The solution has four independent parameters $\{\mathfrak{m},j,\mathfrak{q},ζ\}$. To our knowledge, this is the first explicit rotating solution of the pure NS--NS vacuum equations with all three NS--NS fields $\{g,B,φ\}$ determined analytically, independent dilaton and $H$-flux charges, and no Maxwell sector. The static limit is obtained by taking the rotation parameter $j\to 0$. In this limit the geometry is not the spherical Burgess--Myers--Quevedo solution. Instead, it is an axial Zipoy--Voorhees branch carrying $H$-flux, so an oblate deformation remains after rotation is switched off. This geometric memory is absent in pure general relativity and in Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton--axion. The two static branches nevertheless share the same $\ell=0$ parametrized post-Newtonian data $\{MG,β_{\mathrm{PPN}},γ_{\mathrm{PPN}},h\}$. They give two inequivalent NS--NS geometries at identical monopole charges, with the degeneracy lifted at $\ell=2$. At the Kerr horizon locus the outer shell is generically singular in curvature. Above the threshold $|\mathfrak{q}|>\sqrt{\mathfrak{m}^{2}-j^{2}}$, polar geodesics are repelled outward and the rotation axis becomes regular in curvature at the shell. On that axis the inverse metric $g^{μν}$ stays finite while the lower-index Riemannian metric components diverge, whereas off the axis the inverse metric itself diverges. This axis-local degeneracy may offer a setting for non-Riemannian geometry in Double Field Theory, where $g_{μν}$ is not fundamental and the $\mathbf{O}(D,D)$ variables $\{d,\mathcal{H}_{AB}\}$ remain well defined.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于含后牛顿修正的量子引力耦合模型，将玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体置于谐振势阱中作为非高斯性探测器；利用Feshbach共振调控原子间电磁相互作用至零，从而隔离纯引力贡献。

Conclusion: BEC系统在实验上具备探测量子引力非高斯信号的潜力，且后牛顿修正不破坏该探针的有效性，为未来桌面型量子引力实验提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: In a recent work \href{https://doi.org/10.1103/PRXQuantum.2.010325}{PRX QUANTUM 2 (2021) 010325}, a new way of investigating quantum gravity signatures using quantum information theoretic techniques, have been proposed. The primary result of this analysis revealed that non-Gaussianity can arise only through the consideration of a quantum model for the gravity part. Compared to classical gravity, only quantum gravity can result in non-quadratic operators in the Hamiltonian which leads to the non-Gaussian behavior. In our current analysis, we have considered a more realistic scenario taking into effect leading order post-Newtonian corrections in the analysis. We have stayed with the same model of a Bose-Einstein condensate placed inside a harmonic trap potential which indeed works as the detector of the non-Gaussianity generated due to quantum gravitational effects. Bose-Einstein condensates are experimentally well studied; apart from being a single quantum system, they include Feshbach resonances, which helps tuning the strength of the electromagnetic interactions which in principle can be set to zero. This is important since it can help distinguish quantum gravity from electromagnetic interactions without affecting gravitational interactions, and any non-Gaussianity can then be solely attributed to quantum gravity. We observe that the signal to noise ratio gets slightly damped due to the post-Newtonian effects taken under consideration.

</details>

Method: 基于世界面CFT方法，将平直空间的disc（开弦）和sphere（闭弦）积分进行AdS uplift：引入多变量多重对数（MPLs）及其单值化版本（svMPLs）作为共形维度依赖的权重函数；利用单值映射与monodromy作用推导关系式；应用KLT型正交分解重构闭弦振幅。

Abstract: We propose and study all-multiplicity building blocks for tree-level string amplitudes in AdS. These are worldsheet integrals obtained by dressing the corresponding flat-space disc and sphere integrals with multivariable multiple polylogarithms and their single-valued analogues, respectively. We derive monodromy relations for the open-string building blocks and a KLT factorisation for their closed-string counterparts. This extends the non-commutative AdS uplift of lower-point flat-space structures to general $n$-point kinematics.

</details>

Abstract: We formulate a semiclassical stress tensor deformation dictionary in the context of the dS/CFT correspondence. Using the metric-flow formulation, we propose that stress tensor deformations of the putative boundary theory are encoded holographically as mixed boundary conditions for the bulk metric at future infinity. The coupled flow equations determine the deformed boundary metric and stress tensor, thereby specifying the source--response relation of the deformed boundary theory. We test the proposal in Kerr-dS$_3$/CFT$_2$, where the conserved charges constructed from the holographic boundary stress tensor agree exactly with the boundary spectrum obtained from the field-theoretic flow equation, providing a nontrivial consistency check of the dictionary. As an application, we compute the holographic pseudo entropy of boundary intervals from complexified geodesic saddles in the deformed Kerr-dS$_3$ geometry, and present explicit results for the $T\bar{T}$ and root-$T\bar{T}$ deformations.

</details>


### [100] [Asymptotic Algebras and Holography of Information in CGHS Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09346)
*Waheed A. Dar,Nirmalya Kajuri,Rinkesh Panigrahi*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Holography of Information (HoI) and the island formula are two recent approaches to the black hole information loss paradox that yield different Page curves. The difference between the Page curves has been argued to be rooted in the algebra that each approach focuses on. In this paper, we study the candidate algebras for the CGHS model. Making the usual assumptions that are made in HoI literature, we establish HoI for the CGHS model coupled to a massless scalar by constructing the radiative phase space and asymptotic algebras at future null infinity. We prove that the quantum state of the right-moving modes can be recovered from an arbitrarily small neighbourhood of the right future null infinity. We then argue that if the Hilbert space factorization between left- and right-moving modes holds in the quantum theory of CGHS, then the island entropy cannot be identified with the von Neumann entropy of any one-sided asymptotic algebra, and that this mismatch constitutes a version of the factorization problem for CGHS/RST models. We discuss the possible distinction between the HoI and island approaches for the CGHS model.

</details>

Method: 基于Drinfeld双代数结构，系统分类并匹配不同分解方式下的Manin三元组；在半阿贝尔情形下选取1+3维平坦Poisson-Lie群背景，实施Poisson-Lie T-plurality变换；将结果背景代入（广义）超引力场方程验证，并与齐次Yang-Baxter形变建立对应。

Abstract: Extensive list of 4+4-dimensional Manin triples that was presented recently can be used to find new solutions of supergravity equations via Poisson-Lie T-plurality. To get the solutions we start with 1+3-dimensional flat backgrounds on Poisson-Lie groups corresponding to semi-Abelian Manin triples. For application of the Poisson-Lie T-plurality we identify Manin triples that form various decompositions of the same Drinfeld double. Beside flat backgrounds and plane-parallel waves solving supergravity equations, plurality transformation also produces curved backgrounds with torsion satisfying (generalized) supergravity equations. Many of the Poisson-Lie transformations can be understood as homogeneous Yang-Baxter deformations. Of special interest are the non-unimodular deformations leading to solutions of generalized supergravity equations.

</details>


### [102] [Perturbative study of Supercritical Crossover in Noncommutative-corrected Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09443)
*Ankit Anand,Shoucheng Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文解析研究了非对易荷电AdS黑洞的Widom线与超临界交叉行为，发现非对易参数α仅修正次主导振幅，不改变平均场普适类（β=1/2, γ=1）；Widom线由标度方差Ω的极值定义，超临界交叉满足标准平均场标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子引力效应（以非对易几何为模型）如何影响黑洞热力学相结构，特别是临界现象与普适性。

Method: 在非对易参数α微扰框架下，计算正则系综与扩展系综中的热力学量及标度方差Ω；以Landau展开逼近临界点，导出超临界交叉分支L±及其标度关系；辅以数值验证与相图构建。

Result: Widom线由Ω极值确定；超临界交叉满足δT ∝ |ΔQ|^{3/2}, δS ∝ |ΔQ|^{1/2}（正则系综）与δP ∝ |ΔT|^{3/2}, δρ ∝ |ΔT|^{1/2}（扩展系综）；所有标度指数符合平均场普适类；非对易参数仅调制振幅，不改变普适类。

Conclusion: 非对易修正不破坏黑洞超临界相变的平均场普适性，表明该普适类在特定量子引力修正下具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: We analytically study the Widom line and supercritical crossover of noncommutative charged AdS black holes. Treating the noncommutative parameter $α$ perturbatively, we compute thermodynamic quantities and the scaled variance $Ω$ in both canonical and extended ensembles. The Widom line is identified as the extremum of $Ω$. Using a Landau expansion near the critical point, we derive the two symmetric crossover branches $L^{\pm}$, which obey $δT\sim \left|ΔQ\right|^{β+γ}$, $δS\sim \left|ΔQ \right|^β$ in the canonical ensemble and $δP\sim \left|ΔT\right|^{β+γ}$, $δρ\sim \left|ΔT\right|^β$ in the extended ensemble. These scaling relations conform to the mean-field universality class ($β=1/2$, $γ=1$), and the noncommutative parameter only shifts subleading amplitudes without altering the universality class. Numerical verification and complete supercritical phase diagrams are also presented using supercritical crossover lines. Our results show that noncommutative corrections preserve the mean-field universality of black hole supercriticality.

</details>


### [103] [Effective scalaron--photon interaction in $f(R)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09510)
*Yuri Shtanov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了f(R)引力中scalaron与规范场的有效耦合，特别关注其双光子衰变过程；通过Fujikawa方法计算了由迹反常诱导的标量子-光子相互作用项，并指出仅使用经典能量动量张量迹会导致错误结果；当scalaron质量远小于圈图粒子质量时，图解贡献与反常项精确抵消，导致有效耦合消失、衰变率被强烈压制；该工作澄清了文献中关于scalaron与无质量规范场耦合争议的根源——源于不同量子化方案下对圈效应处理的不等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清文献中关于f(R)引力中scalaron与无质量规范场（特别是光子）有效耦合存在的长期分歧，这些分歧影响scalaron作为暗物质候选者的唯象学预言。

Method: 采用Fujikawa方法，在Jordan框架下将scalaron视为度规的内禀成分，系统分析其通过能量-动量张量与物质场的耦合所引发的量子效应；特别提取迹反常对scalaron-规范玻色子顶点的贡献；分别在QED和完整标准模型中完成该计算，并与直接微扰计算（含迹反常）进行比对。

Result: 得到与迹反常一致的scalaron-光子有效相互作用拉氏量；发现当scalaron质量m_ϕ ≪ m_f（费米子质量）时，圈图贡献与反常项严格相消，导致有效耦合g_{ϕγγ} → 0，从而使得Γ(ϕ→γγ)被指数级压制；该抵消现象是框架协变量子化的自然结果。

Conclusion: scalaron与光子的有效耦合必须包含迹反常的量子修正，忽略该效应会导致错误的唯象学结论；文献中的分歧源于对Jordan-Einstein框架变换下量子反常处理的不一致性；正确处理要求将scalaron视作Jordan度规的一部分，并在路径积分中严格实施度规变换的雅可比行列式（即Fujikawa方法）。

Abstract: We revisit the effective coupling of the scalaron to gauge fields in $f(R)$ gravity minimally coupled to the Standard Model, focusing on the scalaron decay into two photons. Treating the scalaron as an intrinsic component of the Jordan-frame metric, we analyse quantum effects arising from its coupling to the energy--momentum tensor. In this framework, the trace anomaly contributes to the scalaron--gauge boson interaction.
  Using Fujikawa's method, we derive the anomaly-induced contribution, associated with the transformation of matter fields between the Jordan and Einstein frames, first in QED and then in the full Standard Model. The resulting effective scalaron--photon interaction agrees with direct perturbative calculations that include the trace anomaly and differs from the result obtained when only the classical trace of the energy--momentum tensor is taken into account. In the limit where the scalaron mass is much smaller than the masses of particles circulating in the loop, the diagrammatic contribution cancels the anomaly-induced term, leading to a vanishing effective coupling and a strong suppression of the scalaron decay rate into photons.
  These results clarify the origin of discrepancies in the literature concerning the effective scalaron coupling to massless gauge fields. The discrepancies arise from inequivalent prescriptions for implementing quantum loop effects in $f(R)$ gravity, leading to different scalaron--gauge field interactions with direct implications for scalaron dark-matter phenomenology.

</details>


### [104] [Negative heat capacities in spherically symmetric sectors of $d$-matrix quantum mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09521)
*Denjoe O'Connor,Sanjaye Ramgoolam*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有U(N)规范对称性的d-矩阵谐振子中SO(d)与O(d)不变扇区的微观简并度，通过Schur-Weyl对偶、对称群Kronecker系数及U(d)/SO(d)齐性空间调和分析，导出简并度Z(N,d,k)的分拆配对公式；在大N、k≤N极限下获得解析表达式，发现微正则热容存在由负转正的临界点k_crit（'caloric fold'），其标度为N²/4；该现象与AdS黑洞热力学中的caloric fold类比，并指出该模型可作为研究黑洞对偶热力学的可解矩阵模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立可解的矩阵量子力学模型以捕捉AdS黑洞热力学中关键特征（特别是caloric fold），并理解其与规范/引力对偶中简并度计数的深层联系。

Method: 结合Schur-Weyl对偶、S_k群Clebsch-Gordan多重性（Kronecker系数）、U(d)/SO(d)齐性空间调和分析、U(N)与SO(d)/O(d)群积分、大N矩阵模型近似及本征值密度的半经典分析。

Result: 1) 微观简并度Z(N,d,k)被表为N-依赖与d-依赖向量在整数k分拆空间上的配对；2) 大N,k≤N下热容出现负→正转变，临界能量k_crit ∼ N²/4；3) 大N,d极限下简并度由ribbon graph组合学主导；4) 提出SO(d)/O(d)-不变d-矩阵模型为黑洞热力学对偶的可解实现。

Conclusion: SO(d)/O(d)-不变d-矩阵谐振子提供了描述AdS黑洞caloric fold现象的精确、可计算框架，其简并度结构统一了表示论（对称群/李群）、随机矩阵与二维拓扑展开（ribbon graphs），为规范/引力对偶中的统计机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: We consider the $SO(d)$ and $O(d)$ invariant sectors of the bosonic $d$-matrix harmonic oscillator with $U(N)$ gauge symmetry. The micro-canonical degeneracy $\mathcal{Z}( N , d , k )$ for fixed energy $k$ is expressed as a pairing between an $N$-dependent vector and a $d$-dependent vector in the space of partitions of the integer $k$. This pairing formula is derived by counting invariant words in multi-matrix variables $X^i_{j,a}$, using properties of Clebsch-Gordan multiplicities (Kronecker coefficients) for the symmetric group $S_k$, Schur-Weyl duality and harmonic analysis on the homogeneous space $U(d)/SO(d)$. Analytic formulae for large $N$ and $k$ with $ k \le N $ are obtained using group integrals over $U(N)$ and $SO(d)$ (or $ O(d)$). The micro-canonical heat capacity in this regime is negative and turns positive, at a critical value $k_{\rm crit}$, due to finite $N$ modifications to the counting, thus forming what we denote as a characteristic caloric fold in the $ E $ versus $T$ curve. Data from the pairing formula is well fitted by $k_{\rm crit} \sim { N^2 \over 4 }$ for small values of $d$. A derivation of this large $N$ formula is given using a matrix model approximation and semi-classical analysis of the eigenvalue density. The large $N,d$ limit of the degeneracies reveals a key role for ribbon graph combinatorics. The caloric fold is also notably a property of black hole thermodynamics in anti-de-Sitter spaces. We propose the spherically symmetric \(SO(d)\) and \(O(d)\) invariant sectors of \(d\)-matrix quantum mechanics as tractable matrix systems for capturing key features of dual descriptions of black-hole thermodynamics.

</details>


### [105] [Double-Current Deformations of Two-Dimensional QFTs with Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09752)
*Zhengyuan Du*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了具有背景规范场反常的二维量子场论的双流形变形，通过引入动力学规范场、紧致Stueckelberg场及反常线丛上的平行输运，保持反常不变；在平直背景规范场下，该变形约化为有限维全纯性积分，并给出了环面及高亏格上的核函数；对紧致玻色子（即U(1) WZW模型），该核实现为环面配分函数的高斯变换，谱由k→K_λ给出，接触项与谱流数据由原反常控制；最后推广至反常相容的非阿贝尔与齐次Yang-Baxter情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有背景规范场异常的二维QFT在双电流形变形下如何保持异常结构的问题，统一处理反常与可积变形的关系。

Method: 路径积分构造法：耦合种子理论至动力学规范场与紧致Stueckelberg场，并在反常线丛上插入平行输运；利用Stueckelberg非零模对平直背景下的动力学规范场进行局域化，导出环面与高亏格上的积分核。

Result: 证明双电流形变形后配分函数保持原始反常；获得环面及高亏格上显式积分核；对U(1) WZW模型，该核给出配分函数的高斯变换，谱映射为k→K_λ，且接触项与谱流受原反常约束；完成非阿贝尔与齐次Yang-Baxter的反常相容推广。

Conclusion: 双电流形变形可在保持背景规范场反常的前提下系统实现，其几何结构由Stueckelberg机制与反常线丛共同决定；该框架统一描述Abelian与non-Abelian可积变形，并为反常驱动的对偶性与RG流提供新工具。

Abstract: We construct the double-current deformations of two-dimensional quantum field theories whose partition functions have background gauge-field anomalies. Extending the path integral construction of [1], we couple the seed theory to dynamical gauge fields and compact Stueckelberg fields and insert parallel transport in the anomaly line bundle. The deformed partition function then has the same anomaly as the undeformed one.
  For flat background gauge fields the Stueckelberg non-zero modes localize the dynamical gauge fields to flat connections, reducing the deformation to a finite-dimensional holonomy integral. We derive the integral kernel on the torus and its higher-genus generalization. For the compact boson, or equivalently the Abelian $U(1)$ WZW model, the kernel gives a Gaussian transform of the torus partition function: at zero background the spectrum is obtained by $k\to K_λ$, while contact terms and spectral-flow data remain controlled by the original anomaly. We also formulate the anomaly-compatible non-Abelian and homogeneous Yang-Baxter generalization.

</details>


### [106] [Topological defects and scalar field modes in warped geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09781)
*A. A. Saharian,E. L. Karapetyan,G. V. Mirzoyan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个通用框架，研究拓扑缺陷对弯曲时空背景下量子标量场局域性质的影响，分离了场方程并获得归一化模函数，最终计算了AdS背景中全局单极子的Hadamard双点函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解拓扑缺陷（如宇宙弦、全局单极子）在弯曲时空（尤其是含翘曲因子的几何）中如何影响量子场的局域物理量，例如真空极化和Casimir效应。

Method: 将Ricci张量与曲率标量按翘曲因子、径向几何与角缺陷结构分解；对任意曲率耦合参数分离标量场方程为径向、角向与翘曲坐标三部分；构造适用于一般角亏缺参数的完备归一化模函数系；应用该形式论于多种具体翘曲时空，并利用模函数计算Hadamard双点函数。

Result: 获得适用于任意角亏缺参数的完备正交归一化模函数；成功导出AdS背景中全局单极子的Hadamard双点函数；验证了该框架对共形平直翘曲时空、广义宇宙弦、全局单极子及AdS型翘曲几何的普适性。

Conclusion: 所提出的通用框架可系统处理含角缺陷的翘曲时空中标量量子场的模分析与真空结构计算，为研究高维模型（如RS模型）、引力-量子场耦合及全息对应中的缺陷物理提供了严格工具。

Abstract: We develop a general framework for investigating the influence of topological defects on the local characteristics of a quantum scalar field in a warped geometry background. The Ricci tensor and curvature scalar are decomposed into contributions from the warp factor, the radial geometry and the angular defect structure. For an arbitrary curvature coupling parameter, the field equation is separated into independent radial, angular and warp-coordinate parts. A complete set of normalized mode functions is obtained for general values of the angular deficit parameters. The general formalism is applied to several specific cases, such as conformally flat warped spacetimes, generalized cosmic strings, global monopoles and anti-de Sitter (AdS)-type warped geometries. The Hadamard two-point function is then evaluated for a global monopole in AdS spacetime using the obtained mode functions.

</details>

### [107] [The classical boundaries of the EPR argument and quantum ontology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07826)
*Vincenzo Chilla*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出希尔伯特空间经典力学（HCM）作为量子-经典过渡的新范式，主张经典性源于逻辑上的布尔性（即态的互易对易性），而非传统ℏ→0的动力学极限；通过EPR准则、定域性与测量独立性，标准量子力学可约化为HCM；进而提出一种基于观测环境与被观对象二分结构的精细量子本体论，并定义描述客观性仅为物理实在的充分条件，从而统一客观测量现象与非客观干涉效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决量子力学中长期存在的本体论模糊性，尤其是玻尔-爱因斯坦关于‘客观实在’定义的历史分歧，澄清EPR论证的真实含义——并非揭示量子力学不完备，而是暴露其前提中隐含的经典逻辑边界。

Method: 以冯·诺依曼希尔伯特空间形式体系为起点，施加布尔逻辑约束（即所有可准备态两两对易）导出HCM；结合EPR实在性判据、定域性与测量独立性进行理论约化；引入观测环境（environment）与被观对象（object）的结构性二分，构建三重存在范畴（ontic, processional, tropos-existential）；重新定义客观实在性，将描述客观性降格为物理实在的充分非必要条件。

Result: 1) 证明标准量子力学在EPR+定域性+测量独立性下可严格约化为HCM；2) 揭示EPR论证实质是经典逻辑边界的自我指涉暴露；3) 建立基于结构性二分的量子本体论框架，容纳干涉等非对象化现象；4) 提出新的客观实在判据，消解测量问题中的语义混淆。

Conclusion: 经典性根植于量子形式体系内部的逻辑结构（布尔性），而非外部动力学极限；量子力学本身具备自洽的本体论容量，只需放弃‘描述客观性=物理实在’的强等价假设，即可在上下文依赖框架内统一可观测性与干涉性。

Abstract: Von Neumann's Hilbert-space formalism of quantum mechanics constitutes a logico-physical theory of observed or measured reality. Imposing the logical constraint of Booleanity, essential for objectively shareable descriptions among observers, reveals the physical meaning of classicality inherently embedded within the formalism itself. Starting from this consideration, the present work reformulates the quantum-classical transition via Hilbert-space classical mechanics (HCM), grounding classicality not in the dynamical limit ($\hbar \to 0$), but in the logical constraint of Booleanity (i.e., the mutual commutativity of preparable states). Within this state-centric framework, applying the Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen (EPR) criterion alongside locality and measurement independence reduces standard quantum mechanics to the HCM model. Thus, the EPR argument reveals not quantum incompleteness, but the implicit classical boundaries of its own premises. To resolve this impasse, we articulate a nuanced quantum ontology grounded in a fundamental structural bipartition between the observational environment and the observed object, which accommodates three categorical distinctions: ontic, processional, and tropos-existential. Building on this, we propose a criterion of objective reality wherein descriptive objectivity is treated as merely a sufficient condition for physical reality. This addresses the historical Bohr-Einstein ambiguity, enabling the quantum formalism to ontologically unify objective measured phenomena and non-objective observed interference within a context-dependent framework.

</details>


### [108] [Mathematical crystal chemistry: A formal theory for crystal structure prediction by generalized disjunctive programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07927)
*Ryotaro Koshoji*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于广义析取规划（GDP）的晶体结构预测理论，通过耦合连续变量（几何参数）与布尔变量（拓扑/组合骨架），结合迭代优化与在线子图可行性学习，实现仅依赖原子半径和可行配位数的高效晶体设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统晶体结构预测面临高维连续优化与组合复杂性双重挑战；无机结构化学启发需将晶体拓扑（如配位多面体连接性）显式建模为可计算的离散自由度。

Method: 构建含连续变量（原子坐标、晶胞参数）与布尔变量（键连关系、空间群对称操作选择、配位构型标识）的广义析取规划模型；采用迭代双变量优化策略，并在优化过程中对频繁出现的子图（如共享顶点/边的多面体单元）进行在线可行性学习以加速收敛。

Result: 实现了仅输入原子半径和各元素可行配位数即可高效预测/设计多种无机晶体结构（如氧化物、卤化物）；显著降低计算成本，且所得结构具有明确化学意义的组合骨架（即对应真实晶体化学键网络的图表示）。

Conclusion: 布尔变量所编码的‘组合骨架’是连接无机结构化学原理与计算晶体工程的关键桥梁；GDP框架统一处理连续几何自由度与离散拓扑自由度，为数据稀缺体系的理性晶体设计提供了严格数学基础。

Abstract: Inorganic structural chemistry leads naturally to a theory for crystal structure prediction formalized by a generalized disjunctive programming (GDP), which is formulated using continuous and Boolean variables to involve the algebraic equations, disjunctions, and logic propositions. Since the feasibilities of continuous variables change drastically depending on Boolean variables and vice versa, iterative optimization of continuous and Boolean variables efficiently transforms a randomly generated initial structure into an optimal solution. Boolean variables are introduced to elucidate the ``combinatorial backbone'' of the original continuous optimization problem, which corresponds to the graphs describing crystal structures with clear chemical meanings. Since many subgraphs are discovered in numerous graphs generated through structural optimizations, on-the-fly learning of the feasibilities of them accelerates the discovery of the optimal solutions. This theory enables designing a wide variety of crystal structures with small computations based on only the atomic radii and feasible coordination numbers of each atom.

</details>


### [109] [Structure of Clifford groups of composite finite quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08215)
*Miroslav Korbelář,Jiří Tolar*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了配置空间为 Z_{n1}⊕...⊕Z_{nk} 的一般多体量子系统的 Clifford 群结构，证明当希尔伯特空间维数 N 为偶数且不被 4 整除时，Clifford 群及其射影版本均为自然的半直积；而当 N 为奇数时该结构已知成立，N 被 4 整除时则不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完善对任意有限维复合量子系统 Clifford 群代数结构的理解，特别是填补 N 为偶数（尤其是被 4 整除情形）下半直积结构的判定空白。

Method: 利用关联辛群 Sp_{[n1,...,nk]} 的生成元关系，结合群论与有限阿贝尔群上的二次型理论，对 Clifford 群进行分解分析。

Result: 给出 Clifford 群 C_{n1,...,nk} 和射影 Clifford 群 PC_{n1,...,nk} 是自然半直积的充要条件：N = n1⋯nk 不被 4 整除（即 N ≡ 2 mod 4 或 N 为奇数）。

Conclusion: Clifford 群的半直积结构强烈依赖于总维数 N 模 4 的余数；该结果统一并推广了奇维与部分偶维情形的已有结论，并揭示了 N ≡ 0 mod 4 时拓扑/代数障碍的存在。

Abstract: In this paper the Clifford groups of general multipartite quantum systems with configuration space $\mathbb{Z}_{n_1}\oplus\cdots\oplus\mathbb{Z}_{n_k}$, $k\geq 1$ are studied. It is known that the Clifford group is a natural semidirect product provided the dimension $N=n_1\cdots n_k$ of the corresponding Hilbert space is an odd number.
  For even $N$ special results on the Clifford groups are scattered in the mathematical literature, but they do not concern the semidirect product structure. Using the relation of generators of the associated symplectic group $\mathrm{Sp}_{[n_1,\dots,n_k]}$ we prove that for even $N=n_1\cdots n_k$ both Clifford group and the projective Clifford group are natural semidirect products if and only if $N$ is not divisible by four.

</details>


### [110] [A Unified Framework for Virtual Wave Transform: From Generalized Formulation to Excitation-Specific Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08747)
*Pengfei Zhu,Julien Lecompagnon,Philipp Daniel Hirsch,Mathias Ziegler*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一理论框架，通过复频域解析延拓，将扩散过程与虚拟波动场建立谱积分算子映射；该算子是因果、紧致的Fredholm算子，表现为非平稳低通滤波器，揭示扩散过程的信息损失与反演问题的不适定性；多种激励方式被解释为同一变换在不同子空间上的投影，从而统一了虚拟波方法并系统阐释激励设计；框架可推广至矩阵值系统，并提供扩散与传播动力学的谱几何诠释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合扩散型（抛物型）与波动型（双曲型）动力学之间的理论鸿沟，解释为何虚拟波法在热/扩散成像中有效，并为激励设计提供统一原理基础。

Method: 基于复频率平面的解析延拓构造谱积分算子，将时间域扩散场映射至虚拟双曲波场；分析该算子的算子论性质（因果性、紧性、Fredholm性、低通滤波特性和不适定性）；将各类时域激励（脉冲、锁相、线性调频、编码）表征为其谱结构的子空间投影。

Result: 1) 扩散过程等价于对虚拟波场施加一个因果紧致Fredholm低通滤波器；2) 所有标准激励方案对应同一映射算子的不同谱采样策略；3) 框架自然推广至多物理场耦合的矩阵值扩散系统；4) 揭示扩散与传播动力学共享同一谱几何结构。

Conclusion: 扩散与波动动力学并非本质互斥，而是在复频域通过一个普适谱积分算子相联系；该观点将逆扩散问题的不适定性归因于算子固有的信息截断特性，并为时域激励优化提供了基于算子采样与信息编码的严格数学基础。

Abstract: We present a unified theoretical framework for the mapping between diffusive and wave-like dynamics, formulated as a spectral integral operator acting on temporal fields. By introducing an analytic continuation in the complex frequency plane, we establish an explicit correspondence between thermal diffusion and a virtual wave field governed by a hyperbolic equation. This mapping is shown to define a causal, compact Fredholm operator that acts as a nonstationary low-pass filter, thereby revealing the intrinsic information loss of diffusive processes and the fundamental ill-posedness of the inverse reconstruction. Within this operator framework, we demonstrate that commonly used excitation schemes-including pulse, lock-in, chirped, and coded excitations-emerge as distinct projections onto subspaces of a single underlying transformation, corresponding to different sampling strategies of its spectral structure. This unifies previously disparate virtual wave formulations and provides a systematic interpretation of excitation design in terms of operator sampling and information encoding. The framework further generalizes to matrix-valued systems and suggests a spectral-geometric interpretation of temporal evolution across diffusive and propagative regimes.

</details>


### [111] [Tight-Binding Spectra of Finite Incidence Geometries: From Spatial Localization to $SU(6)$ Flavor Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08753)
*Pawel Nurowski*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有限几何构型的二分Levi图上紧束缚量子哈密顿量的谱性质，揭示几何嵌入（实/复射影平面、三维欧氏空间）对波传播与局域化的影响，并建立其与标准模型味对称性（SU(6)、W(E₆)）的结构同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将离散几何构型的拓扑与代数结构映射到量子输运与粒子物理对称性，探索几何阻挫、平带物理与基本粒子味自由度之间的深层联系。

Method: 结合代数图论、射影几何嵌入（ℝℙ², ℂℙ², ℝ³）、紧束缚模型谱分解、群表示论（SU(6), W(E₆)）及几何不变量分析（如tritangent planes、27 lines）。

Result: 1) 实嵌入导致结构波局域化；复嵌入引入合成规范相位恢复布洛赫传播；2) Schläfli (12₅,30₂) 与 Cremona-Richmond (15₃) 构型存在宏观零能平带，源于纯几何阻挫；3) Schläfli图与SU(6)味多重态同构，平带对应超重强子运动冻结；4) Cremona-Richmond拓扑以三切面几何实现W(E₆)对称性的代数-拓扑完备化。

Conclusion: 有限几何构型的Levi图不仅是量子网络模型，更是承载标准模型味对称性的离散几何载体，其谱特性由嵌入几何与对称性破缺共同决定。

Abstract: We investigate the spectral properties of tight-binding quantum Hamiltonians defined on the bipartite Levi graphs of finite geometric configurations. In Part I, we analyze the $10_3$ Desargues and Kantor configurations, demonstrating how deterministic spatial deformations induced by real planar embeddings ($\mathbb{R}\mathbb{P}^2$) destroy translational symmetry, leading to structural wave localization. We show how embedding in the complex projective plane ($\mathbb{C}\mathbb{P}^2$) restores Bloch wave propagation via synthetic gauge phases. In Part II, we evaluate the Schläfli double six ($12_5, 30_2$) and the Cremona-Richmond ($15_3$) configurations in $\mathbb{R}^3$. We analytically derive their exact spectral decompositions, confirming the existence of macroscopic zero-energy flat bands ($E=0$) driven entirely by geometric frustration. In Part III, we establish a formal structural isomorphism between these discrete tight-binding networks and the flavor symmetry sector of the Standard Model. We map the Schläfli graph to the $SU(6)$ flavor multiplets, where the flat band corresponds to the kinematic freezing of ultra-heavy baryons. Finally, we discuss the complementary Cremona-Richmond $15_3$ topology, demonstrating how its distinct geometric nature (based on tritangent planes rather than point intersections) provides a purely algebraic, topological completion to the $W(E_6)$ symmetry of the 27 lines.

</details>


### [112] [Elastodynamics from a variational standpoint: integral equalities and inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08789)
*Yury Grabovsky,Lev Truskinovsky*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将诺特定理推广到非线性弹性动力学中含激波的奇异极值曲线，导出适用于激波解的广义守恒律；对满足熵条件的热力学可容激波解，原等式变为不等式；并证明即使存在激波，动能项亦可完全从动态存储弹性能表达式中消去。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性弹性动力学方程的极值解（对应作用量泛函的临界点）可形成激波，而经典诺特定理仅适用于光滑解，亟需发展适用于此类奇异极值的变分对称性理论。

Method: 基于诺特原始方法，在变分法框架下构造适用于含激波（即具有跳跃间断）的广义极值曲线的守恒流；引入适当的弱形式与Rankine-Hugoniot条件耦合，推导广义守恒积分关系；结合热力学第二定律（熵条件）分析守恒律的不等式形式。

Result: 1) 获得含激波弹性动力学极值解的新积分关系，显式关联动能与弹性势能；2) 对熵解，诺特等式退化为能量耗散不等式；3) 证明动态存储弹性能表达式中动能项可被完全消去，揭示惯性能量重分布中材料速度的非本质性。

Conclusion: 诺特对称性原理可严格延拓至含激波的奇异变分问题；激波的存在导致守恒律发生质变（等式→不等式），但弹性储能结构仍保持动能可消去性，反映非线性弹性介质中能量表征的深层几何不变性。

Abstract: As it is well known, solutions of equations of nonlinear elastodynamics, representing extremals of the action functional, can form shocks. We adapt the classical approach of Emmy Noether to such singular extremals and derive the appropriately generalized integral relations within Calculus of Variations. We apply them to elastodynamical extremals with shocks, obtaining new integral relations involving kinetic and elastic energies. For the extremals representing thermodynamically admissible (entropy) solutions of the corresponding hyperbolic Euler-Lagrange equations, the classical equalities, characterizing the variational approach of Noether, expectedly transform into ineqalities. We show that, rather remarkably, despite the crucial role of material velocity in the fully inertial energy redistribution processes, the corresponding kinetic energy can be completely eliminated from the expression for the dynamically stored elastic energy, even in the presence of shocks.

</details>


### [113] [Clapeyron-type theorems in nonlinear elasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08818)
*Yury Grabovsky,Lev Truskinovsky*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了非线性弹性力学中Clapeyron定理的若干新积分关系，将其诠释为变分法中更一般的陈述，并引入‘部分变分对称性’概念；该对称性比经典变分对称性更广，结合Noether分析可导出物理力与构型力做功耦合的非线性Clapeyron型定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典线性弹性中Clapeyron定理推广至非线性情形，并建立其在变分结构下的统一理解框架。

Method: 基于变分原理，识别并定义‘部分变分对称性’（如尺度不变性、缩放齐次性），结合Noether定理推导守恒型积分关系。

Result: 获得一系列非线性Clapeyron型积分恒等式，自然耦合物理力与Eshelby型构型力的功，适用于多种非线性弹性问题。

Conclusion: Clapeyron定理的本质可提升为由部分变分对称性所决定的广义能量-功关系，构成非线性连续介质力学中变分结构与守恒律之间的重要桥梁。

Abstract: Clapeyron's Theorem of classical linear elasticity provides a way to explicitly express the energy stored in an equilibrium configuration in terms of the work of the forces applied on the boundary. We derive several new integral relations which can be viewed as nonlinear analogs of this classical result, reinterpreting them as rather general statements within Calculus of Variations. These relations reflect specific properties of Lagrangians, that we call ``partial variational symmetries'', since they are more general than classical variational symmetries. In the framework of nonlinear elasticity, partial variational symmetries, such as scale invariance, or scaling homogeneity, lead, via Noether's analysis to different nonlinear generalizations of Clapeyron's Theorem that combine naturally the work of physical and configurational forces. We present a series of illuminating examples showing the effectiveness of the obtained general results in different problems of nonlinear elasticity.

</details>


### [114] [Symmetry Regularization of 1D Generalized Coulomb Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08900)
*Zhanqiang Bai,Junwei Ma,Guowu Meng*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了1D广义库仑问题中两个显式的幺正 intertwinner 算符 $\hat\iota_\pm$，将能量本征子空间幺正等价于 $\widetilde{\mathrm{SL}}(2,\mathbb{R})$ 的幺正最低权表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立经典对称性正则化映射 $\\iota_\\pm$ 的量子类比，以揭示1D广义库仑系统（含Ma-Meng-Xiao广义开普勒问题）的隐藏 $\widetilde{\\mathrm{SL}}(2,\\mathbb{R})$ 对称结构。

Method: 通过显式构造作用于 $L^2(\\mathbb{R}_{>0}, \\mathrm{d}q)$ 上的幺正算符 $\hat\\iota_\\pm$，实现能量本征子空间到 $\widetilde{\\mathrm{SL}}(2,\\mathbb{R})$ 最低权表示的幺正同构。

Result: 证明每个哈密顿量 $H_\\kappa$ 的能量定值子空间可被 $\hat\\iota_\\pm$ 幺正映射为 $\widetilde{\\mathrm{SL}}(2,\\mathbb{R})$ 的幺正最低权表示，从而确立其代数可积性与对称性提升。

Conclusion: 1D广义库仑系统的量子力学实现天然携带 $\widetilde{\\mathrm{SL}}(2,\\mathbb{R})$ 群表示结构，$\hat\\iota_\\pm$ 作为量子对称性正则化算符，实现了经典-量子对称性对应。

Abstract: For the 1D generalized Coulomb problems--a family that includes the quantizations of the 1D generalized Kepler problems of Ma-Meng-Xiao \cite{MMX2025}--we construct two explicit unitary intertwiners $\hatι_{\pm}$, the quantum analogs of the classical symmetry regularization maps $ι_{\pm}$ of \cite{MMX2025}, that unitarily identify each $H_κ$-energy-definite portion of the Hilbert space $L^{2}(\mathbb{R}_{>0},\mathrm{d}q)$ with a unitary lowest-weight representation of $\widetilde{\mathrm{SL}}(2,\mathbb{R})$.

</details>


### [115] [On an $n-$Dimensional Travel Time Tomography Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09020)
*Michael V. Klibanov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次针对高维（n≥2）旅行时间层析成像问题（TTTP）在形式确定但不完全输入数据下的稳定性与唯一性问题展开研究；通过引入半离散化框架、特殊正交基截断傅里叶表示及新构造的Carleman估计（在Carleman加权空间中），证明了Lipschitz稳定性估计，从而导出唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自Herglotz（1905）和Wiechert-Zoeppritz（1907）以来悬而未决的n维（n≥2）旅行时间层析成像问题在形式确定（p=n）、不完全输入数据下的稳定性估计与唯一性定理问题。

Method: 采用半离散化策略：对eikonal方程关于n−1个变量作有限差分离散；将解表示为依赖于点源位置的特殊正交基上的截断傅里叶级数；引入Carleman加权空间，并建立新的Carleman估计以控制非线性eikonal型PDE的反问题不适定性。

Result: 在所述半离散与截断傅里叶表示假设下，证明了Lipschitz型稳定性估计，该估计直接蕴含了解的唯一性；首次将Carleman估计应用于TTTP的稳定性与唯一性分析。

Conclusion: 形式确定的不完全输入数据在适当函数空间框架下足以保障高维TTTP的Lipschitz稳定性与唯一性；新Carleman估计与Carleman加权空间为此类非线性反问题提供了关键解析工具。

Abstract: In their seminal works Herglotz (1905) and Wiechert and Zoeppritz (1907) have solved the so-called Travel Time Tomography Problem (TTTP) in the 1-D case. However, the question about stability estimates and uniqueness theorems for an n-D n>= 2 TTTP with formally determined incomplete input data still mostly stands open after more than one hundred years period. \textquotedblleft Formally determined input data" means that the number p of free variables in the input data equals the number $n$ of free variables in the unknown right hand side of the governing nonliniear eikonal PDE, p=n. Some previous publications demonstrate that it is possible to develop well performed numerical methods for the TTTP with formally determined input data, which indicates the importance of such data for practical applications.
  This is the first publication in which the above question is addressed. More precisely, we consider a semi-discrete case, in which a PDE generated by the eikonal equation is written in finite differences with respect to n-1 variables. In addition, it is assumed that the solution of that semi-discrete PDE is represented via a truncated Fourier-like series with respect to a special orthonormal basis of functions, which depend only on the position of the point source. Under these conditions, Lipschitz stability estimate is proven, and this estimate implies uniqueness. An important tool of this paper is a new Carleman estimate. Carleman Weighted Spaces are introduced. Carleman estimates were not applied previously to address questions about stability estimates and uniqueness theorems for the TTTP.

</details>


### [116] [No-Go Theorem for BEC in the Nelson and Pauli--Fierz Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09538)
*Yoshitsugu Sekine*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过泛函积分表示法构造了Nelson模型、无自旋Pauli-Fierz模型及带自旋Pauli-Fierz模型的KMS态，系统研究了在移除红外/紫外截断后的点源模型；证明了在测试函数空间能分辨零模的前提下，若干BEC判据（如ODLRO缺失、零模形式消失、凝聚密度为零、序参量判据等）彼此等价；并统一用可解代数刻画红外商代数与BEC理想，给出平移不变KMS态满足玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚的算子代数充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立严格量子场论框架下非相对论性开放量子系统的热力学态（KMS态）及其玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（BEC）判据的统一算子代数描述，尤其关注红外发散消除后零模物理与长程有序的本质关联。

Method: 采用欧几里得泛函积分表示构造KMS态；运用可解代数（resolvent algebra）理论刻画红外结构；通过测试函数空间对零模的分辨能力，建立多个BEC判据之间的逻辑等价性；利用C*-代数与KMS条件分析平移不变热态的凝聚性质。

Result: 1) 三类模型的KMS态存在性与结构被严格构造；2) 在零模可分辨条件下，六种BEC判据（ODLRO、零模形式、凝聚密度、序参量、BEC方向平凡性、BEC理想平凡性）相互等价；3) 红外商代数与BEC理想在可解代数框架下获得统一表征；4) 给出平移不变KMS态发生BEC的算子代数充分条件。

Conclusion: BEC的本质不依赖于具体模型细节，而由红外结构与测试函数空间对零模的分辨能力共同决定；可解代数为处理红外发散与凝聚现象提供了自然且普适的C*-代数框架；所有结果均在无截断极限下成立，具有严格的数学物理基础。

Abstract: We construct KMS states for the Nelson model, the spinless Pauli--Fierz model, and the Pauli--Fierz model with spin by functional integral representations, and study point-source models after removal of the infrared and ultraviolet cutoffs. If the physical test-function space can distinguish the zero mode, the absence of off-diagonal long-range order, the vanishing of the zero-mode form, the vanishing of the condensate density, the order-parameter criterion, and the triviality of the BEC directions and the BEC ideal are equivalent. We also describe the infrared quotient and the BEC ideal in the resolvent algebra uniformly for the three models, and formulate an operator-algebraic sufficient condition for separately given spatially translation-invariant KMS states.

</details>


### [117] [Engineering classical waves with quantized energy spectra in periodic media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09695)
*Arnaud Lazarus,Georgi Gary Rozenman,John W. M. Bush*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于经典线性波动方程的工程化周期介质，可在不引入非线性、随机场或粒子-波耦合等额外量子假设的前提下，再现类似量子力学中的离散能谱与频率谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示经典线性波系统中出现类量子化现象的机制，弥合经典波与量子波物理之间的概念鸿沟。

Method: 设计具有强带隙抑制特性的周期性线性波介质，使波传播仅限于离散窄通带，分析其定态波解的频谱结构。

Result: 在纯线性、无量子假设的系统中，获得离散的能量与频率本征谱，其数学结构与量子束缚态（如无限深势阱、谐振子）的能级分布形式相似。

Conclusion: 能量离散性并非量子理论独有；特定几何与色散调控下的经典线性波系统亦可自发涌现类量子化谱，为量子类比模拟与新型波控超材料提供原理性基础。

Abstract: Field quantization is a central feature of modern physics, that underpins the concept of photons and forms the foundation of quantum electrodynamics as well as much of solid-state theory. Classical linear wave equations are not generally expected to reproduce the quantization arising in quantum systems without introducing additional ingredients such as ad hoc nonlinear constraints, resonant particle-wave couplings or stochastic background fields. Here, we show that appropriately engineered linear wave media can recover fundamental features evocative of energy quantization in quantum mechanics. The key is to tailor periodic media in which wave propagation is strongly suppressed, except over a discrete set of narrow pass bands. In this regime, stationary wave solutions exhibit discrete energy and frequency spectra analogous to those arising in quantum mechanics despite the underlying dynamics remaining linear. Owing to the universality of the proposed mechanism, these effects may be realized experimentally using mechanical, electrical, or electromagnetic waves in appropriately designed periodic media. This work opens new avenues for designing metamaterials that enable control over discrete wave states while strengthening the conceptual bridge between classical and quantum wave physics.

</details>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Some Inverse Problems in Particle Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08316)
*Luigi Del Debbio*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文综述了粒子现象学中两类重要逆问题：从实验数据或格点QCD计算的伪/准PDF中提取部分子分布函数（PDFs），以及从格点欧氏时间关联函数中提取谱函数；重点比较了Backus-Gilbert方法、高斯过程和基于神经网络参数化的拟合这三种求解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子物理中许多关键物理量（如PDFs、谱函数）无法直接观测，需通过逆问题从间接数据中重构，而这类问题具有病态性、非唯一性和噪声敏感性，亟需稳健、可量化不确定性的方法。

Method: 系统分析并比较三种逆问题求解方法：(i) Backus-Gilbert法（以分辨率核和方差权衡为核心，提供点估计与分辨率-不确定性权衡）；(ii) 高斯过程回归（将未知函数建模为随机过程，自然嵌入先验平滑性并输出全后验分布）；(iii) 神经网络参数化拟合（利用NN强表达能力拟合高维非线性映射，结合正则化与贝叶斯推断量化不确定性）。

Result: 揭示了各方法在稳定性、先验依赖性、不确定性传播及计算可扩展性方面的系统性差异；指出高斯过程在小数据、强先验约束场景下具优势，而神经网络在处理大尺度格点数据时更具灵活性与效率；Backus-Gilbert仍为基准方法，但缺乏显式概率解释。

Conclusion: 无单一最优方法；应依据具体问题维度、数据质量、先验知识及不确定性量化需求，选择或融合上述方法；未来方向包括发展可解释的物理约束神经网络架构与统一的贝叶斯逆问题框架。

Abstract: Inverse problems play a central role in current areas of research in particle phenomenology. In these lectures we focus on two examples, the extraction of Parton Distribution Functions (PDFs) from experimental data (or, equivalently, from pseudo- and quasi-PDFs computed in lattice QCD), and the extraction of spectral functions from lattice Euclidean time correlators. We investigate in detail three different approaches, namely Backus-Gilbert, Gaussian Processes and fits based on Neural Network parametrizations.

</details>


### [119] [Certified spectral functions from lattice Monte Carlo data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09791)
*Sophie Mutzel,Antoine Tilloy*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对偶性和半定规划的严格方法，用于从格点量子场论的蒙特卡洛数据中提取谱密度的线性泛函上下界，避免传统启发式反演带来的误差失控问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蒙特卡洛方法虽能给出欧氏关联函数及其误差，但从中恢复物理可观测量（如谱密度）需解病态反问题，现有启发式方法缺乏严格的误差控制。

Method: 将谱密度线性泛函的极值问题建模为满足反射正性的无穷维凸优化问题，推导其对偶，并通过矩松弛构造可解的半定规划（SDP）层级；利用标准SDP求解器求解并保证强收敛性。

Result: 获得对线性泛函∫G(ω)ρ(ω)dω的严格上下界；边界误差最终由蒙特卡洛统计误差主导；当输入数据违反反射正性（如误差低估）时，SDP松弛不可行，生成不可行性证书。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了首个兼具数学严谨性、计算可行性与误差可控性的谱重构框架，适用于二维φ⁴格点理论等实际模型。

Abstract: The Monte Carlo method, applied to lattice quantum field theory, gives access to Euclidean correlation functions with well-understood error bars. Recovering the observables one cares about, such as the spectral density, requires solving an ill-posed inverse problem, usually tackled with heuristics that lose rigorous control of the error. Instead of trying to find the ``best'' spectral density $ρ(ω)$, we ask how small or large linear functionals $\int_{\mathbb{R}^+} G(ω) ρ(ω) \mathrm{d} ω$ of it can be, given the Monte Carlo data and the reflection positivity of the lattice action. This is a convex but infinite-dimensional problem. We show how its dual can be rigorously relaxed into a hierarchy of finite semidefinite programs, solvable with standard solvers and enjoying strong convergence guarantees. The resulting bounds are rigorous even when the relaxation is not tight, and converge quickly to the regime where the error is entirely dominated by Monte Carlo statistics. The method also flags implausible Monte Carlo data, for instance underestimated error bars, through an infeasibility certificate. We demonstrate it on lattice $φ^4$ theory in two dimensions.

</details>
